## ⏱ AMENDMENT **#153-A** — 2026-08-11, POST-WRAP (OPUS amendment sub, after commit `c5b406f`, Dave live — ⛔ **THE "ENVIRONMENTAL FENCE" BELOW WAS FALSE, AND THE BINDING OBSTACLE WAS A RUNBOOK NOBODY OPENED** · ✅ **`s152-D1` IS ENACTED *AND VERIFIED* — rc=0, 57 ARMS, RE-DRIVEN FIRST-HAND HERE** · ✅ **THE TWO STAR REDS ARE *MEASURED CLEAR*, NOT MERELY DECLARED KNOWN-FALSE** · ⚠ **AN ARM BIT ON ITS FIRST REAL RUN AND THE BITE WAS THE ARM'S** · ⛔ **THE OLD BLOCKER IS REPLACED BY A NARROW, MEASURED ONE: THE CALL BOUNDARY** · ⛔ **STILL RULES NOTHING**)

⚠ **ADDITION ONLY. Every word of the `#153` stratum below stands unaltered as the record of what that wrap believed and why** [[header-wins-over-audit]]. This section corrects it; it does not replace it.

- ⛔ **THE ENVIRONMENTAL FENCE WAS FALSE — AND THIS IS THE FINDING, MORE THAN THE FIX IS.** The `#153` wrap inscribed *"chromium would not launch … `libXdamage.so.1` … no root"* as an environmental fact. It is not one. `knowledge/_RUNBOOK-render-verify.md` **`:181`** stages the missing libs with **`apt-get download`** — and **`:186`** says in as many words *"(`apt-get download` needs no root and no `apt-get update`.)"*. The lib farm was **ALREADY ON DISK THE WHOLE TIME**: `/var/tmp/chromelibs/root/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libXdamage.so.1`, in a directory dated **Aug 8 15:21** — three days before `#153`. ⚠ **Verified first-hand here:** the brief relayed the farm's path as `/var/tmp/chromelibs`; at that level there are only `.deb` files, and the extracted `.so`s live one `root/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu` deeper — **that path IS the `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` the runbook prints at `:44`**, so the correction is a precision, not a reversal. Runbook **`:36`** names the exact error committed: *"(`PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH` alone) was NOT sufficient this time; the full working env, all four."* The `#153` conductor set `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH`, hit the lib error, tried `apt-get install` (root, refused), and declared a fence — **without opening the runbook that answers it** [[feedback-read-the-runbook]].
- ★ **THIS IS THE THIRD INSTANCE OF ONE CLASS, AND DAVE CAUGHT IT BOTH TIMES WITH ALMOST THE SAME SENTENCE.** `#123` declared a render gap on *"chromium is TLS-blocked in-sandbox"*; `#124` carried that forward **as fact**; Dave stopped it with *"and there is a runbook for chromium and playwright"* — his words are inscribed at `_RUNBOOK-render-verify.md` **`:131`**, and the runbook's own opening at **`:5`** records the same ask (*"there should be a runbook for chromium and playwright"*). Today, at `#153`, he said *"I think theres a runbook for chromium and playwright"*. ⛔ **Three instances — `#123`, `#124`, `#153` — and on all three the agent's own refusal was sincere, named, and about the WRONG obstacle.** The runbook existed each time; the cost each time was that nobody opened it [[refusal-names-the-first-obstacle]].
- ✅ **`s152-D1` IS ENACTED *AND VERIFIED*. RE-DRIVEN FIRST-HAND BY THIS SUB, NOT RELAYED.** With the runbook's full env (`PYTHONPATH=/var/tmp/pysite` · `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH=/var/tmp/pw-browsers` · `LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/var/tmp/chromelibs/root/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu` · `TMPDIR=/var/tmp` · `PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_VALIDATE_HOST_REQUIREMENTS=1`), `python3 _validate_state_contrast.py --selftest` → **rc=0, `"selftest OK — 57 arms"`, zero bites**, `grep -c '^  ok '` = **57**. All five `s152-D1` arms green, **including `arm_removing_the_skip_brings_the_phantom_back`** — the mutation control that cuts `if(fg&&bodyPaints){` out of MEASURE and demands the 1.662 return [[mutation-tests-the-clause-not-the-feature]]. Conductor's receipt: `/sessions/<s>/mnt/outputs/s153-selftest-final.txt`; this sub's independent re-drive is the claim of record.
- ✅ **THE TWO SELECTION-CONTROLS STAR REDS ARE CLEARED *WITH A MEASUREMENT*, NOT WITH AN ARGUMENT.** `V.run(['Selection-controls'])` driven live here: **MARK text failures 0** (was **2**) · **other text failures 0** · total TEXT failures **0**. They are no longer *known-false-and-declared*; they are **measured clear**. ⚠ **The audit artefact was deliberately NOT written** — `run()` measures, `main()` writes (`:1206`); `knowledge/_STATE-CONTRAST-AUDIT.md` mtime held at **2026-08-11 15:38:59, 2,364 bytes, 21 lines**, byte-identical **before and after every drive in this session — checked, not assumed**. ⇒ **the artefact on disk is now KNOWN-STALE: it still prints the 2 reds that no longer reproduce.** Declared, not silently tolerated [[enactment-register-adr-0016]].
- ⚠ **UNIT CORRECTION — THE "28 SKIPS" IS A *RAW* COUNT, AND THE REPORT WOULD PRINT *4*.** The brief relayed **28 declared MARK SKIPs**; re-measured here, `Selection-controls` yields **42 raw records → 28 raw `markskip`s → 4 UNIQUE `markskip` lines** after `render_report`'s own de-duplication key (`:632`–`:635`). Both numbers are true of different things; **the audit reports the de-duplicated one**. Naming which is which is the whole point [[measure-dont-convert-units]].
- ⚠ **AN ARM BIT ON ITS FIRST REAL RUN, AND THE BITE WAS THE ARM'S — NOT THE MECHANISM'S.** `arm_browser_phantom_shape_fill_is_skipped_and_declared` asserted the reason string *"no painted fill at all"*. The branch that actually fires is the **DECLARED-BUT-UNWORN** one, because an `<svg>` with no `fill` attribute computes to **`rgb(0, 0, 0)`** — which is **not `none`** — so `shapeFillStr` is truthy. Corroborated first-hand against the conductor's pre-fix receipt `s153-selftest-runbook-env.txt`, which carries the bite verbatim. Remedy in `knowledge/_validate_state_contrast.py`: assert the phantom colour **explicitly** (`fill == "rgb(0, 0, 0)"`) plus `"no descendant shape paints"`, so the arm can no longer pass against the other branch by accident, with the reason inscribed in a comment above it. ★ **A bite that names its own assertion is the arm working** [[a-crash-is-not-a-fail]].
- ★ **AN OBSERVATION ABOUT THE LEG'S REAL REACH — AND IT RULES NOTHING.** 28 raw (4 unique) declared skips on **one** snippet says the MARK leg was only ever measuring anything real on **genuine roundels**; its former red count **overstated its reach**. Recorded as an observation only. ⛔ **No rule, no scope change, no ruling is derived from it here** — `s152-D1`'s own open item (*"whether any other snippet in the 75 hits the same class — unmeasured"*) stays open and unmeasured.
- ⛔ **THE OLD BLOCKER IS RETIRED AND REPLACED BY A NARROW, MEASURED ONE: THE CALL BOUNDARY, NOT THE BROWSER.** A full sweep of `_validate_state_contrast.py` **exceeds the ~178 s tool-call cap** (the conductor's run was killed at **177,998 ms**). ⚠ **THE BRIEF SAID "38 SNIPPETS" AND THAT NUMBER IS WRONG — MEASURED HERE: `knowledge/snippets/*.reference.html` = **75**,** which is also the figure `s152-D1`'s own open item already uses. Timed first-hand on a 3-snippet drive: **49.5 s ⇒ 16.5 s/snippet ⇒ ~1,237 s (~20.6 min) for 75** — roughly **7× the cap**, not marginally over it. ⇒ **the audit cannot be regenerated in one call and needs chunking in the shape of `_build_all.py --range/--resume`** [[sandbox-call-boundary-kills]]. The browser is fine; **the call boundary is the limit** — and unlike the fence it replaces, this one is measured.
- ⚠ **CORRECTING THE PRIOR WRAP'S OWN RESIDUAL ①.** It said the arms have *"no runner in the sandbox OR in CI"*. **They DO run in the sandbox**, via the runbook — proven above. What remains true is that **CI still excludes them**: `.github/workflows/gates.yml` **`:20`–`:22`**, re-read first-hand and quoted — *"The render-dependent checks (`_validate_state_contrast.py`, `_validate_screen.py --render`) are NOT run here — they need a browser engine."* ⇒ **`s152-D1`'s proof now has a runner in the sandbox but still none in CI.**
- ⬛ **PROPOSAL FOR #154 — NOT A DECISION, AND NOT RULED HERE.** A CI render job is now **cheap and live**: `gates.yml` runs on `ubuntu-latest`, which **has root**, where `playwright install --with-deps chromium` works without any of the lib-farm choreography. That would give the browser arms a runner in CI as well as in the sandbox. ⛔ **Put to Dave as a proposal; his call, not this sub's** [[feedback-dont-launder-a-premise-into-a-ruling]].
- ⛔ **THIS AMENDMENT RULES NOTHING.** `s153-D1` (green/success-ink) stays **FLOATED, AWAITING DAVE'S CONFIRMATION** — the SHAPE only, **the two values are NOT ruled and are NOT invented here**, and it stays in `_FUTURE-STATE.md` and **deliberately out of `knowledge/_rulings.json`**. Untouched and carried unchanged: the **icon-leg phantom residual** (declared, unfixed) · all **22** of Dave's open G-items · every ratified stratum (**ADD, never trim**) · the CI job (proposal only). ⛔ **NOT PUSHED** — push only on Dave's explicit word, not given to this sub.

Dossier addendum: `_DECISION-HISTORY/2026-08-11-153-the-phantom-fill-and-the-refusal-that-named-the-wrong-obstacle.md` § **AMENDMENT #153-A**.


## ⏱ PRIOR DELTA — 2026-08-11 (Tue **#153**, OPUS conductor + this OPUS wrap sub, Dave live, ONE ARC — enact `s152-D1`: fix the gate's MARK leg — ⚠ **SUPERSEDED IN PART BY AMENDMENT #153-A ABOVE — the "ENACTED, NOT VERIFIED" verdict and the environmental fence it rests on are CORRECTED THERE; this stratum stands unaltered as the record of what was believed** — ✅ **THE FIX LANDED AND IT KEYS ON *PAINTED*, NOT ON THE COLOUR BLACK** · ✅ **THE SKIP IS DECLARED, NEVER SILENT — AND NO RATIFIED COUNT MOVES** · ✅ **3 FIXTURES · 4 ARMS · A MUTATION CONTROL THAT DEMANDS THE 1.66 BACK** · ⛔ **ENACTED, *NOT VERIFIED* — EVERY BROWSER-DRIVEN ARM IS UNRUN, IN THE SANDBOX *AND* IN CI** · ★ **A REFUSAL NAMES THE FIRST OBSTACLE, NOT THE BINDING ONE** · ⛔ **THE TWO STAR REDS STAY KNOWN-FALSE AND DECLARED** · ⛔ **THE ICON LEG SHARES THE PHANTOM — DECLARED, NOT FIXED** · ⛔ **`s153-D1` FLOATED, NOT CONFIRMED** · ⛔ **QUOTA POLL UNANSWERED**)

**Landed (evidence per claim):**

- ✅ **THE FIX — THE MARK LEG NOW RUNS ONLY ON A FILL SOMETHING ACTUALLY WEARS.** `knowledge/_validate_state_contrast.py`, new `shapeFillStr` + `bodyPaints` probe inside the `<svg>` branch of MEASURE (**`:336`–`:355`, verified first-hand by this sub**): a descendant matching `path,circle,rect,ellipse,line,polyline,polygon,use,text,tspan`, **not** inside `defs,clipPath,mask,symbol,marker,pattern`, **not** hidden/`display:none`/`opacity:0`, whose **computed fill string equals the svg's**. If nothing wears it, the comparison is **SKIPPED**. ★ The presence test compares **COMPUTED STRINGS, never `parse()`** — parsing every descendant would MINT REFUSALS this change was not ruled to create, and the delta would stop being attributable [[attribute-the-diff]]. ★ **It keys on PAINTED, not on the colour black:** a genuine black roundel still fails, and that is a fixture, not a promise.
- ✅ **THE SKIP IS DECLARED, NEVER SILENT — AND IT MOVES NO COUNT DAVE RATIFIED.** New `kind:'markskip'` record (`where`/`fill`/`reason`); new `MARKSKIP_PREFIX = "- 🟡 MARK SKIP (declared, s152-D1)"` at **`:512`**, **deliberately NOT one of the counted prefixes**; surfaced in `render_report` as a section bit and a body line. **`verify_report`'s three counter contracts are UNTOUCHED — verified first-hand, not relayed:** the entire diff (**+127/−5**) REMOVES only two lines, `if(fg){` and the old `_measure_fixtures()` signature. ★ A gate that quietly stops reporting is indistinguishable from a gate that was fixed [[enactment-register-adr-0016]].
- ✅ **3 FIXTURES · 4 ARMS · A MUTATION CONTROL.** Fixtures at **`:832`/`:842`/`:849`** — `mark_phantom_fill_is_skipped`, `mark_real_black_roundel_still_fails` (**the discriminating one**), `mark_body_only_in_defs_is_skipped`. Arms at **`:1114`–`:1156`**. `_measure_fixtures` gained `script=MEASURE, only=None` (**`:856`** — defaults keep every existing call identical). The **mutation control cuts `if(fg&&bodyPaints){` → `if(fg){` out of MEASURE**, re-drives the phantom fixture and **DEMANDS the 1.66 reappear**; a guard arm fails loudly if the target is not found [[unmatched-grep-is-not-an-absence]] [[mutation-tests-the-clause-not-the-feature]].
- ⛔ **ENACTED, *NOT VERIFIED* — AND THIS IS THE HEADLINE.** Every **browser-driven** arm is **UNRUN**. `python3 -m py_compile` rc 0. `--selftest` **RE-DRIVEN FIRST-HAND BY THIS WRAP SUB — rc=2**, refusing loud and named: `StateContrastSelftestError: chromium would not launch … libXdamage.so.1: cannot open shared object file`. No root (dpkg lock), disk **97% / 360M free** (re-measured — **worse** than the 95%/499M relayed). **The gate REFUSING is it working as designed, and it is NOT a pass.** ⚠ **AND CI DOES NOT RUN IT EITHER** — `.github/workflows/gates.yml:20`–`:22`, read first-hand: the render-dependent checks are explicitly excluded. ✅ The **FIX** does have a runner, confirmed first-hand: `_build_all.py:255` (gate) and `:261` (`--selftest`). ⇒ ★ **A sharper instance of [[instrument-without-a-consumer]]: the FIX has a consumer; the PROOF does not.** Only Dave's local render path can close it.
- ⛔ **THE TWO SELECTION-CONTROLS STAR REDS STAY *KNOWN-FALSE AND DECLARED* — NOT GREEN.** They clear when the arms are driven, not when the code is written.
- ★ **NEW FINDING — A REFUSAL NAMES THE *FIRST* OBSTACLE, NOT THE *BINDING* ONE.** The same command, twice, both `rc=2`, **two DIFFERENT named refusals**: without `PYTHONPATH=/var/tmp/pysite` it reports *"the 'playwright' module is not installed"* — **false about the disk** (playwright **1.62.0** IS at `/var/tmp/pysite`; browsers at `/var/tmp/pw-browsers`). A session running only that form would have inscribed a true-sounding falsehood about its own environment, from an honest gate. Cause: **bash calls carry no env, so the install survives on disk while the PATH TO IT does not survive a call boundary** [[sandbox-call-boundary-kills]]. ★ **AND 18 PURE ARMS RUN AND PASS IN-SANDBOX TODAY** (`grep -c "^  ok "` = 18, in **both** runs) — first-hand corroboration that residual ② is cheap and real. But `--selftest` is rc=2 overall, so those greens are **not reported as a pass**.
- ★ **THE CONDUCTOR'S CATCH, INSCRIBED BECAUSE IT GENERALISES.** A redirect to `/var/tmp/st.out` hit permission-denied, and `tail` then read a **LEFTOVER FILE FROM AN EARLIER SESSION** whose *"selftest OK — 25 arms"* line was nearly reported as this run's result — caught only because the `s151-D1` carrier arm names were **absent** from it. ★ **A FAILED WRITE PLUS A SUCCESSFUL READ LOOKS EXACTLY LIKE A SUCCESSFUL RUN** [[ritual-output-is-not-evidence]] [[stale-mount-corroborates-a-stale-premise]]. Remedy applied, and re-applied by this sub on both its own runs: **write to a path this session owns, and print mtime-vs-now beside the verdict.**
- ⛔ **DECLARED RESIDUAL, DELIBERATELY NOT FIXED — THE ICON LEG SHARES THE PHANTOM.** `kind:'icon'` at the same site still uses the same phantom `fg`. It is **WARN-only** and **outside `s152-D1`'s ruled scope**, so it was not touched and is **not ruled here** [[gate-glob-scope-rule]].
- ★ **SECOND ENOSPC DATAPOINT — A DATAPOINT, NOT A RUNBOOK REWRITE.** `pip install` to the default prefix died on ENOSPC; `TMPDIR=/var/tmp pip install <pkg> --break-system-packages --target=/var/tmp/pysite` **SUCCEEDED**. The `/var/tmp/pw-browsers` cache **survived** from a prior session but is unusable without the missing system lib. The open ENOSPC RUNBOOK CORRECTION carry stays open, now at n=2.
- ⛔ **`s153-D1` — GREEN/SUCCESS-INK SCOPE: FLOATED, *NOT CONFIRMED*.** Dave picked *"mirror the two-red law"* from an option set and the conductor **reflected it back**; he has **not confirmed**. The **SHAPE** (a background-keyed fork) is what he indicated; ⛔ **the two values are NOT ruled and must not be invented** — deriving a green pair from `s151-D1`'s red pair would be exactly the laundering this record exists to prevent [[feedback-dont-launder-a-premise-into-a-ruling]]. Recorded in the **floated register `_FUTURE-STATE.md` ONLY**, deliberately **not** in `knowledge/_rulings.json`.
- ⚙ **GAUGE — RELAYED BY THE CONDUCTOR, NOT RE-MEASURED BY THIS SUB, AND SAID SO.** boot **54,723** real, inside the 54,859±1,178 band — a datapoint, **never corrected into the constant** [[boot-floor-measured-109]]. Check-ins at the **opener, MID-LANE (FILL 111,538) and WRAP-OPEN (FILL 140,364 vs stop 150,929 — inside)** [[checkin-at-the-ends-cannot-catch-the-lane]]. Delegation: **this OPUS wrap sub only**, binding budget named as **FILL, not quota** [[delegation-cost-inversion-110]].
- ⛔ **QUOTA POLLED AT THE OPENER, NOT ANSWERED — an UNANSWERED POLL is NOT a stale reading.** #150's stands: session **7%** · weekly **52%** · Fable **65%** [[quota-panel-has-three-numbers]].
- ⚠ **THE 2d EXIT CHECK BIT, AND IT IS WORTH THE LINE.** Two items on the rolling **#150** delta — *generators write by default, no `--dry-run`* and *the token-drift class itself* — had **no standing home anywhere in the corpus** (probed: hits in exactly one place, the delta about to roll). Both **copied up to `_FUTURE-STATE.md` BEFORE the roll**. Their three siblings did have homes and were **not** copied [[gap-in-record-vs-gap-in-evidence]].
- ⛔ **THIS WRAP RULED NOTHING.** Untouched: the icon leg · the two star reds' status beyond *known-false, declared* · all **22** of Dave's open G-items · every ratified stratum (**ADD, never trim**) · every prior #152 carry, rolled forward with ages incremented. ⛔ **NOT PUSHED** — push only on Dave's explicit word, not given to this sub.

**Landed (evidence per claim):**

- ✅ **`s145-D1` RULED AND ENACTED IN THE SAME WINDOW, DAVE'S.** The plus/minus rung `s144-D1` left unnamed is **`rag/<severity>-ink`** — minted `rag/error-ink` (light `#DA1A00` / dark `#F6604C`) and `rag/success-ink` (light `#137F3C` / dark `#66CC8D`) in `knowledge/tokens/semantic-colour.json`; `s144-D1`'s four values unchanged. Dave picked `-ink` from a measured three-option set. `-text` refused because `rag/text/*` is GROUND-keyed (`s131-D1`) while this rung is MODE-keyed with hue as the meaning. Stays under `rag/` because `s144-D1` locks the hue to the `s122-D2` fills. `ink` was already the system's word on the consumption side (`--ink` → `text/default`, sutherland-fixtures.json) but new as a spine path segment.
- ★ **FIRST MODE-FORKED HUE RUNG IN THE RAG FAMILY, FORCED NOT CHOSEN.** All 12 prior rag hue rungs carry one hex both modes; only `-tint` forked before. The two ink rungs fork because `s144-D1`'s arithmetic makes a mode-invariant AA-text value impossible — the file's asymmetry is a receipt of that finding. Only error/success get a rung; warning/information OUT by Dave's word at #144.
- ✅ **ENACTMENT RECEIPTS READ OFF THE GENERATED FILES.** `canon.css` lines 304/309 (light), 659/664 (dark). `semantic-colour.json` by textual insertion, rest parse-equal, 2 new keys (rag leaves 19→21). Regen re-driven: `gen_canon_tokens.py --check` → 543 root vars (+2), 189 dark overrides (+2), byte-identical on re-run · `gen_theme_cascade.py --check` → 201 paths/206 projections, no change · `gen_canon_components.py --check` → 75 components, in sync. `knowledge/_rulings.json` now 105 rulings, `s145-D1` last, 103/104 priors byte-identical, `s144-D1` amended BY ADDITION only. Gates rc=0: `_validate_dtcg.py` PASS 0/61 · `_validate_binds_ratchet.py` PASS 33/75 · `_validate_standing_instructions.py` PASS 28 · `_validate_a11y.py` 0 failures/179 warnings/446 controls+201 marks.
- ⚠ **HONEST GAP: NOTHING CONSUMES THE INK RUNGS YET.** `amount-display.sign`'s enum is `["none","negative"]` — no `positive` value (#143 finding ⑤, never closed) — so `rag/success-ink` has no binding site. AA receipts come from Dave's #144 tuner, not a gate: PROVEN-by-tuner, UNPROVEN-by-gate. Recorded in `s145-D1`'s own `open` array.
- ⬛★★ **LANE ② DISSOLVED — ITS PREMISE WAS STALE, AND THE GAP UNDERNEATH IS WORSE.** Parked #143 and #144 as "colour-spine DTCG migration, 7 rows." Probed before briefing a sub: all 7 addresses already resolve in `semantic-colour.json` to `$value`/`$type` leaves; `_validate_dtcg.py` reports 0 failures on the spine, its 8 deferrals are all `DEF-COLOR-MISTYPE` on blur/image opacity tokens, none on rag/surface. The migration landed at #141 (`s141-D1`, axis A) already — parked twice for a job already done. ⛔ **THE REAL GAP: no gate resolves a meta `binds` address against the colour spine.** Four gates mention `binds`; none opens `semantic-colour.json`. Rename a rung and 7 metas point at nothing while every gate stays green — `no-gate-parses-the-artefact` recurring. Correction applied BY ADDITION to all 7 `$status` strings: `alert.meta.json::status` · `amount-display.meta.json::sign` · `banner.meta.json::status` · `toast.meta.json::status` · `avatar.meta.json::surface` · `button.meta.json::surface` · `divider.meta.json::surface`.
- ⚠ **THIS WRAP'S OWN FINDING: `canon.css`'S FONT-FAMILY FALLBACK WAS SILENTLY DROPPED BY THIS SESSION'S REGEN.** `--typography-font-family-default` now reads bare `Univers Next for HSBC`, both themes, where HEAD carried the full stack `"Univers Next for HSBC", "Helvetica Neue", Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif`. Cause: `gen_canon_tokens.py`'s `FONT_STACK` substitution fires only `if ttype == "string"`, but the token's DTCG type is `"fontFamily"` — never matches; `$webStack` is never read by the generator. `canon.css` had not regenerated since #132, so this sat latent until this session exercised the path. Reproduced on a second `--check` run. Not reverted by this wrap — a generator-code decision outside the remit; named in the residual.
- ⚙ **GAUGE.** boot **55,068** real — inside the 54,859±1,178 band, never corrected into the constant. Check-ins ran INSIDE the lane: 64,809 → 82,124 → 107,460 → 121,942 (FILL). Stop line 150,929; wrap opened at 121,942 — UNDER. Binding budget FILL throughout. One avoidable ~17K spend: a grep for `address-intent` caught `_memento-index.json` and dumped a prior-session banner — same class as #144's ~27K.
- ⛔ **QUOTA NEVER POLLED — SECOND SESSION RUNNING.** Asked at the opener, offered again mid-session; Dave answered the routing question instead. Routing rested on #143's figures (session 19% · weekly 41% · Fable 53%). Sub spend was approved for lane ②, but the lane dissolved before a sub was spawned — this wrap sub is the session's only delegation.
- ✅⚠ **READ-CHAIN HELD, TENTH SESSION** (`_CHAIN.md` only). **ENOSPC n=11** confirms `PYTHONPATH=/var/tmp/py-s142` a third time, zero install; three runbooks still instruct install-first.

## Rolled 2026-08-10 #147 (2d) — via the mover
## ⏱ PRIOR DELTA — 2026-08-10 (Mon **#144**, OPUS conductor + 3 Sonnet subs, Dave live, ONE ARC — the rag a11y lane, residual ① of #143 — ✅ **`s144-D1` RULED: FOUR VALUES, SCOPE COLLAPSED TWICE** · ★ **MODE-INVARIANT AA TEXT ARITHMETICALLY IMPOSSIBLE** · ⛔ **#143'S "NO BARE rag.success" WAS FALSE** · ★ **HUE LOCKS IN ONE SPACE, NOT BOTH** · ⬛ **LANE ② DID NOT OPEN, CONDUCTOR'S CALL** · ⚠ **QUOTA NEVER POLLED** · ✅ **READ-CHAIN HELD**)

**Landed (evidence per claim):**

- ✅ **`s144-D1` RULED, DAVE'S — SCOPE COLLAPSED TWICE.** #143 handed over all four rag glyph rungs failing AA plus an unspecified rung; Dave narrowed to coloured text only, then to red/green plus-minus only, verbatim: "we don't need to worry about info or warning text, they are not used and the only coloured text is for plus and minus, red and green. these are the only coloured text we use." Info/warning coloured text OUT. Rationale for text vs fills, verbatim: "statuses are fine they have labels and the roundels carry a glyph that create teh meaning tah background doesnt matter."
- ✅ **FOUR VALUES**, hue locked to the `s122-D2` fills (red 7.0588°/30.2375°, green 142.9412°/154.5908° HSB/OKLCH), saturation/brightness only: minus/red LIGHT `#DA1A00` (4.503) · minus/red DARK `#F6604C` (4.763, unchanged) · plus/green LIGHT `#137F3C` (4.503) · plus/green DARK `#66CC8D` (7.531, unchanged). Ground set excludes the two rag tints per `s134-D4`. Recorded `knowledge/_rulings.json` § `s144-D1` by the conductor, 103 priors asserted parse-equal, 104 rulings at HEAD.
- ★ **MODE-INVARIANT AA TEXT IS ARITHMETICALLY IMPOSSIBLE** — vs `#FFFFFF` needs L ≤ 0.1833, vs `#1A1A1A` needs L ≥ 0.2215, empty window. This is WHY the coloured-text rung is separate from `s122-D2`'s mode-invariant fills, not an edit to them. Dark needed no new value (fills already pass as text: error 5.55, success 8.77).
- ⛔ **#143'S "NO BARE `rag.success`" WAS A FALSE ABSENCE**, corrected in the record: all four bare roles exist and resolve via `$alias` to their `-glyph` rung. [[unmatched-grep-is-not-an-absence]]
- ★★ **TWO TUNER FINDINGS.** v1's gamut clip moved green-light hue +6.84° while reporting only `gamutClipped:true`; v2's hard chroma-clamp (2.6× the gamut ceiling) held drift to +0.087°. Hue can be locked in ONE colour space, not both — green-light carries −4.53° OKLCH drift at only −0.16° HSB drift; v2's alarm keys to the driving model (HSB) so stayed silent — a property of HSB's non-uniformity, not a defect. Dave accepted it as tuned.
- ★ **THE CONDUCTOR STATED AN ESTIMATE AS A MEASUREMENT** — briefed the locked hues as 7.3°/145.3° vs the actual 7.0588°/142.9412°; the build sub re-derived, caught it, locked to its own measurement. [[planning-estimate-is-not-a-measurement]]
- ⚙ **GAUGE.** boot **54,872** real — inside the 54,859±1,178 band, never corrected into the constant. FOUR instrumental check-ins ran inside the lane — 71,785 → 99,004 → 107,379 → 118,056. One call cost ~27K alone (a full `rag` dump printed to stdout) — the session's biggest avoidable spend. Stop line 150,929; last reading 118,056, UNDER. Delegation: 3 Sonnet subs (237,644 + 262,346 + 263,921 quota) + this wrap sub; binding budget FILL throughout.
- ⛔ **QUOTA NEVER POLLED** — asked three times, Dave never answered; routing rested on #143's day-old figures (session 19% · weekly 41% · Fable 53%).
- ✅⚠ **READ-CHAIN HELD, NINTH SESSION** (`_CHAIN.md` only) · **ENOSPC n=10** confirms the `PYTHONPATH=/var/tmp/py-s142` remedy a second time with zero install; three runbooks still instruct install-first.
- ⬛ **LANE ② (COLOUR-SPINE DTCG MIGRATION) DID NOT OPEN** — the conductor's declared FILL-bound call, Dave informed in advance, not discovered at the wrap. ✅ **Gates RE-DRIVEN by this wrap:** `_validate_dtcg.py` PASS 0 failures/61 deferrals · `_validate_binds_ratchet.py` PASS 33/75 · `_validate_standing_instructions.py` PASS 28 docs.
- ⛔ **THIS WRAP RULED NOTHING AND CLOSED NOTHING BEYOND WHAT `s144-D1` ITSELF RECORDS.** The rung name, the dark selected-row token, the OKLCH-drift acceptance (already Dave's), `tooltip.tip`, the intent/capability vocabularies, the colour-spine migration scheduling, the 42 fork verdicts, the 15 token-split exceptions — all Dave's, all untouched.

Dossier: `_DECISION-HISTORY/2026-08-10-144-the-scope-that-collapsed-twice-and-the-text-rung-arithmetic.md`.

## Rolled 2026-08-10 #141 (2d) — via the mover

**Rolled `Last refreshed` chain tail (2d, same boundary as the delta):**

*Last refreshed: 2026-08-09 (Sun from `date` — **#138 wrap**: ONE lane — **the fontconfig permanent fix**. ✅ **THE CAUSE IS FIXED: the `/var/tmp` symlink farm** — fontconfig's `<dir>` now points at a farm of symlinked repo `.ttf`s rather than the repo directory itself, so its `.uuid`/`.uuid.LCK`/`.uuid.TMP-*` marker lands in `/var/tmp`, never in the tree it scans; ~5 KB of links, preserves the ENOSPC constraint that forced #136's change. Enacted `knowledge/_RUNBOOK-render-verify.md` § SYMLINK FARM (#138), addition-only. Premise reproduced first-hand (`fc-cache` against the old repo-pointed conf wrote the same three stray names #136 left); **driven three ways** — farm conf clean dir → 0 strays · mutation, only `<dir>` swapped back to the repo → 3 strays (test CAN fail) · after cleanup → 0 — **plus a real render run** (`showroom/chart-bar.html`, `goto file://`, 1180+480): `HSBC_MtUnivers_Latin` / `Univers Next HSBC` / `Univers Next for HSBC` all measure **347**, matching each other and NOT the DejaVu (375) or nonexistent-face (301) controls; repo strays after the render: 0. ★ **A FALSE GREEN, CAUGHT AND RECURRING:** the first probe returned 345 for every face including one that does not exist — `FONTCONFIG_FILE` replaces the system config, and with no `<include>` of `/etc/fonts/fonts.conf` only 10 of 394 faces were visible, so every request fell back to the only faces present; a page can render entirely in the HSBC cut and look correct even with a broken fallback, meaning #136-era renders could never have shown one. `<include>` now mandatory, documented; `document.fonts.check()` returned true in both configs and is recorded as worthless. Same class as #137's `sed`-grabbed-comment false green, two sessions running. ✅ **THE CLASS IS NOW GATED:** `instrument_stray_check()`, new in `knowledge/_capture_gate.py`, wired into `wrap_checks()` — two-pass (respects `.gitignore` first, since `knowledge/assets` carries 60 untracked-but-ignored paths; then re-checks WITHOUT `--exclude-standard`, filtered to `.uuid*`, so a `.gitignore` entry cannot blind it), driven four ways incl. the gitignore-cannot-blind-it arm. Lives at the wrap seam, not `_build_all.py` (sandbox-impossible). ⬛ **NOT FULLY CLOSED — the farm is UNPROVEN in a COLD sandbox** (this session re-used `/var/tmp` staging from #129/#131/#136; steps 1–4 never re-run), carried to #139 as residual ①. ⚠ Two potholes hit and documented: ENOSPC n=4 (`/sessions` 100% full, 18 M free, same shape as #129/#136) and a cross-filesystem `mv` (repo→`/var/tmp` fails; same-mount `mv` to `_to_delete/` works), the second caught BY the new gate. ## ⏱ PRIOR DELTA — 2026-08-09 (Sun **#138**, OPUS conductor, Dave live, ONE lane — the fontconfig permanent fix — ✅ **FONTCONFIG OUT OF THE TREE: THE /VAR/TMP SYMLINK FARM** · ★ **A FALSE GREEN CAUGHT AGAIN — TEN FACES READ AS ONE** · ✅ **DRIVEN THREE WAYS + A REAL RENDER** · ✅ **THE INSTRUMENT-STRAY CLASS IS NOW GATED** · ⚠ **ENOSPC n=4 + A CROSS-FILESYSTEM `mv` POTHOLE** · ✅ **QUOTA POLLED** · ✅ **WRAP OPENED BELOW THE LINE**)

**Landed (evidence per claim):**

- ✅ **① FONTCONFIG OUT OF THE TREE — ENACTED AND DRIVEN. Residual ① from #137 is closed except one declared gap.** The permanent fix is a `/var/tmp` **symlink farm**: `ln -s` each repo `.ttf` into `/var/tmp/fonts-<session>/`, point fontconfig's `<dir>` at the farm, so its `.uuid` directory-identity marker lands outside the repo. ~5 KB of links, so it preserves the ENOSPC constraint that forced #136's change rather than reopening it. Enacted in `knowledge/_RUNBOOK-render-verify.md` as a new `§ SYMLINK FARM (#138)` block, addition-only; #129's and #136's blocks stand as history, with a pointer marking the `<dir>`→repo element on `:42`/`:46` SUPERSEDED.
- ★ **② PREMISE REPRODUCED FIRST-HAND BEFORE ANY FIX.** `fc-cache` against a conf whose `<dir>` is the repo TTF dir wrote `.uuid`, `.uuid.LCK`, `.uuid.TMP-SpeXCi` — the same three names #136 left (`.TMP` suffix is random; #136 got `NpSPVs`). `git status --untracked-files=all` flagged all three.
- ✅ **③ DRIVEN THREE WAYS, WITH A MUTATION THAT DISCRIMINATES.** (A) farm conf from a clean dir → **0** repo strays · (B) mutation, only `<dir>` swapped back to the repo, everything else identical → **3** strays, so the test CAN fail · (C) after cleanup → **0**.
- ✅ **④ REAL RENDER RUN, AND THE FACE STILL RENDERS.** `showroom/chart-bar.html`, `goto file://` (`set_content()` is banned) — at 1180 and 480, identical at both widths: `HSBC_MtUnivers_Latin` **347** · `Univers Next HSBC` (type.css `--uf`) **347** · `Univers Next for HSBC` (snippet `--font`) **347** · `DejaVu Sans` control **375** · nonexistent-face control **301**. Both aliases land on the target and NOT on the fallback number. Repo strays after the render: **0**.
- ★ **⑤ A FALSE GREEN CAUGHT BEFORE IT WAS REPORTED — the method finding, and it is a RECURRENCE.** The first probe returned **345 for every single face, including one that does not exist.** `FONTCONFIG_FILE` *replaces* the system config, and the conf had no `<include>` of `/etc/fonts/fonts.conf`, so it exposed **10 faces, all HSBC** (the box has 394). Every request fell back to the only faces present, so a page can render entirely in the HSBC cut and look correct even when its fallback is broken — #136-era renders "verified" pages that could not have shown a fallback bug. The `<include>` is now mandatory and documented. `document.fonts.check()` returned `true` in BOTH the broken and working configs and is therefore worthless as an assertion. Class: [[green-tests-cannot-see-scope]] — same class as #137's `sed`-grabbed-comment-lines false green, two sessions running.

- **★★ DV-D17 — ENACTED · DOM-PROVEN · ✅ RENDER-PROVEN.** `knowledge/_render/verify_dv_d17_render.py`. Licensed HSBC cut asserted **inside the frame being measured**, transitions killed **before the first gesture** (ds-019's lesson, structurally), **real pointer clicks on the real gestures** (LABEL = isolate · SWATCH = check on) — never `classList.add`, which is not a user path. **Six contexts, identical:** snippet @1180/@760 · showroom **light** @1180/@760 · showroom **dark** @1180/@760 (verified genuinely dark — `--ink` `#FFFFFF`, `--line` `#808080` — so it is coverage, not one pane counted twice). **baseline** `rgb(225,225,225)` / `rgba(0,0,0,0)` → **isolated** `rgb(26,26,26)` = `--ink` + `color(srgb 0.101961 … / 0.06)` = 6% ink → **released** back to `rgb(225,225,225)` / `rgba(0,0,0,0)`.
- **★ THE FINDING — A ONE-SIDED PROOF OF AN ABSENCE IS NOT A PROOF.** The specified test was *"no `.dv-legrow` resolves the `.is-solo` treatment after isolate-then-check-on."* That is satisfied by a working fix, a blind probe, a mistyped selector **and a complete revert**. Session #8 ran it and printed `24 checks · 0 failures` while measuring nothing. **The replacement is two-sided in ONE gesture sequence: step 1 proves the treatment CAN be seen (isolate → it paints); step 2 proves it stops.** Step 1 is what makes step 2 mean anything — and it is precisely the half the original lacked. **Generalises past this defect: any proof whose assertion is "X is absent" needs a paired proof that X is detectable when present.**
- **★ THE BITE REPRODUCES DAVE'S SCREENSHOT.** `--bite` on a **neutered COPY** (216-byte DV-D17 release branch deleted; canon untouched, `git status` clean): step 2 fails at both widths with row 1 keeping `.is-solo`, `rgb(26,26,26)` border and 6% ink fill **while three series show** — the black-bordered "B Savings" Dave reported. **Step 1 still passes under the neuter**, which is the whole point: the probe is not blind, the behaviour is broken.
- **★ THE PROBE CAUGHT ITSELF, AND THE RULE IT EXERCISED IS THE ONE INSCRIBED THIS MORNING.** First run went red — *"row 2 paints an ink border with no `.is-solo`"*. **The check was working; the cause was the instrument:** `.dv-legrow:hover` also paints an ink border, and a **real** click leaves the cursor on the row it clicked. Fixed by parking the pointer **and** carrying an OBSERVED `hovered` field, so a hovered row is **named and skipped, never silently filtered**. *(A control that fires may be RIGHT — exhaust that before calling it broken. Here it was right about the pixels and wrong about the cause, and only an observed field could separate the two.)*
- **★★ ds-018 RE-VERIFIED AND IT STANDS — the timing defect did not reach it.** `knowledge/_render/recheck_ds018.py`, settled, pointer parked, 4 contexts: **`--border-disabled` → `''`** · **`--text-disabled` → `''`** · **`border-color` → `rgb(26,26,26)` = `--ink`**. ⚠ **AND IT IS WORSE THAN "DISABLED LOOKS LIKE HOVER" — THE STATES ARE INVERTED:** baseline (Reset **disabled**) → **ink border** · isolated (Reset **enabled and useful**) → `--line` · released (**disabled**) → **ink**. ⇒ **The disabled Reset is the most prominent control in the legend and the enabled one the faintest** — an inversion of affordance, not just a wrong colour. Cause unchanged (the lookup, instance five); **fix shape still Dave's**, and this adds a second cheap gate candidate: *a disabled control may not out-contrast its own enabled state.*
- **Evidence:** `python3 knowledge/_build_all.py` **60/60 GREEN exit 0** · 2026-07-27 · `verify_dv_d17_render.py` green ×6 contexts + `--bite` red ×2 widths · 2026-07-27 · `recheck_ds018.py` 4 contexts · 2026-07-27 · element renders `outputs/_render-env/png/DV-D17-three-states.png` **SEEN** (three legend states, licensed cut) · env staged from cold per `_RUNBOOK-render-verify.md`, **`__dirlock` EPERM appeared exactly as banked — a failure message after a success.**

- **★ ds-019 IS NOT A DEFECT — WITHDRAWN, both beats kept.** `.dv-legrow.is-solo` **(0,2,0), cascade index 4** beats `.dv-legrow` **(0,1,0), index 3**. CDP `CSS.getMatchedStylesForNode`: no `!important`, no inline style, no `attributesStyle`, no keyframes, **no `:is()`/`:where()` list and no `#cb4-legend li` rule.** The "winning rule that does not contain the string `dv-legrow`" **does not exist** — the four-selector census was correct; its premise was false. Confirmed in **six contexts** (snippet 1180/760 · showroom light pane 1180/760 · showroom **dark** pane 1180/760, theme-tracking correctly at `--ink` `#FFFFFF` / 6% white).
- **★ THE MECHANISM — a measured time series, not a reading of CSS.** `.dv-legrow` carries `transition: border-color var(--ease), background var(--ease)` = **0.16s**. before-add `rgb(225,225,225)` / `rgba(0,0,0,0)` · **t=0 same task `rgb(225,225,225)` / `oklab(0 0 0 / 0)`** ← *exactly the pair ds-019 published as proof* · t≈50ms `rgb(145,145,145)` / `oklab(0.217785 … / 0.0241082)` (**mid-interpolation**) · **t≈150ms+ `rgb(26,26,26)` = `--ink` ✓ / `color(srgb 0.101961 … / 0.06)` = 6% ink ✓.** ⚠ **`oklab(…)` serialisation is the signature of an IN-FLIGHT INTERPOLATION, not of a failed declaration.**
- **★ THE TRANSFERABLE FINDING: THE PREDECESSOR'S POSITIVE CONTROL WAS WORKING, AND WAS OVERRULED.** It observed `oklab(0 0 0 / 0)` differing from `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` and passed — and those **are** different states (transitioning-transparent vs static-transparent). Session #8 diagnosed its own control as a string-comparison artefact and dismissed a true signal. The string method was bad **and** its verdict was right. ⇒ **When a control fires unexpectedly, exhaust "it detected something real" before concluding it is broken.** Same family as [[gate-narrows-its-own-rule]] and #7's stale suite — the instrument present, correct-looking, not measuring what it claimed — **but this is the first one INSCRIBED AS A DEFECT, where it then directed work.**
- **✅ DV-D17 UNBLOCKED (still ENACTED · DOM-PROVEN · RENDER-OWED — prove it, do not rebuild it)**; any proof **must settle the transition first** or it reproduces ds-019. **✅ Dave's question answered: the screenshot showed the treatment because the treatment paints. Screenshot right, probe wrong, two sessions. No silent regression, no unprobed context.**
- **★ RULED BY DAVE — RENDER TOOLING GETS A REPO HOME.** `knowledge/_render/cdp_matched_styles.py`. ⚠ The prior handoff's *"the harness is already written — reuse it"* pointed at `outputs/_render-env/probe.py`, and **`outputs/` is `.gitignore` line 45** ⇒ the instruction was **never followable**. New script: CDP cascade enumeration + colours parsed **as colours** + **`--settle off` by default** (injects `transition:none !important` before any class change, so a mid-transition read is structurally impossible); `--settle none` reproduces the artefact on demand. Pothole banked in `_RUNBOOK-render-verify.md`.
- **⚠ ds-018 STANDS, and is NOT re-verified.** Same probe, same session — but a **resting** control, not a post-class-change state, and its census (29 declarations / ten form scopes / **zero** chart scopes) is independent evidence. **"Should not be affected" is not "is not affected" — cheap re-check OWED (§C·4).** Skipped deliberately at ~55% rather than start a fresh measurement hot.
- **Evidence:** `knowledge/_render/cdp_matched_styles.py` · CDP `getMatchedStylesForNode` + `getComputedStyleForNode`, six contexts · 2026-07-27 · time series at t=0/50/150/300/600/1200ms · bite: `.is-solo` deleted from a **copy** reproduces the original numbers exactly, canon untouched · fonts **deliberately not staged** (cascade resolution is font-independent; this measures neither geometry nor paint) · defect `knowledge/_DS-IMPROVEMENTS.md` **ds-019 § WITHDRAWAL block** (original kept verbatim below it).

## ⏱ PRIOR DELTA — 2026-07-27 (Mon **afternoon #8**, OPUS solo self-conducting, effort MAX — ★ **ds-018 CONFIRMED BY MEASUREMENT: it is the LOOKUP, and it is INSTANCE FIVE — a FORM-tier token consumed from a DATAVIZ-tier scope, never resolved since the day it landed** · ★ **ds-019 NEW: `.dv-legrow.is-solo` MATCHES, its vars RESOLVE, and it STILL does not paint — so DV-D17's owed render-proof is VACUOUS and would have read green forever** · ★ **MY OWN PROBE PRINTED 24 CHECKS / 0 FAILURES AND WAS WRONG** — the positive control compared computed colours as STRINGS · **NOTHING BUILT, Dave chose log-and-stop at the fork** · build unchanged **60/60 GREEN**) — "The proof was green because the thing it was watching for has never happened, and could never happen."

- **★ ds-018 — HYPOTHESIS PROVEN, COMPETING EXPLANATION ELIMINATED, BY MEASUREMENT NOT BY READING CSS** (which is what the entry demanded). `getComputedStyle` on the **disabled** Reset, licensed HSBC cut, `document.fonts.check` asserted true FIRST, **four contexts — snippet AND showroom pane (via `page.frames`), at 1180 and 760** — all four identical: **`--border-disabled` → `""` (does not resolve)** · **`--text-disabled` → `""`** · `--ink` → `#1A1A1A` (resolves, so the context is not broken) · **`border-color` → `rgb(26,26,26)` = ink = the `:hover` value** · `color` → `rgb(26,26,26)`. ⇒ IACVT → `border-color` falls to `currentColor`, `color` falls to `inherit`; **both roads end at ink.**
- **★ AND THE CENSUS IS STRONGER THAN THE MEASUREMENT: the token is not set to an ink-ish value, it is NOT SET AT ALL where a chart can see it.** `canon.css` declares `--border-disabled` **29×**, on ten **form** scopes (`.cn-input-fields` · `.cn-dropdown` · `.cn-amount-input` · `.cn-date-picker` · `.cn-date-range-picker` · `.cn-file-upload` · `.cn-form-layout` · `.cn-secure-entry` · `.cn-textarea` · `.cn-time-picker`) + their SC/dark twins — **ZERO on any chart scope, ZERO on `:root`**. The four consuming snippets (`Chart-bar`/`combo`/`donut`/`line`) declare it **0×** and use it 1× — same for `--text-disabled`. ⇒ **A FORM-TIER TOKEN READ FROM A DATAVIZ-TIER SCOPE. It has never resolved on a chart, in any theme, at any width, and every gate was green throughout.**
- **⚠ THE FIX IS WIDER THAN THE SYMPTOM DAVE REPORTED.** `--text-disabled` fails in the same breath and is **masked** (colour is inherited, so it lands on ink, which *reads* plausible) — a fix aimed only at `border-color` leaves a second silent lookup live. Belongs in the **generator + a gate**, never a `canon.css` hand-patch (block is replicated per chart family and regenerated). **Two gate candidates, neither built:** the entry's own *no control's disabled treatment may resolve to its hover treatment* · **and the cheaper, wider one — fail loud when a declaration references a custom property that resolves nowhere in its own scope** (the *fail-loud-on-unknown* shape already ratified for `dv-vocab`). ⚠ **CONFIRMED ≠ FIXED.**
- **★ ds-019 NEW — AND IT IS THE FINDING THAT STOPPED THE WINDOW.** Applying `.is-solo` **directly**, no gestures, no behaviour code: element **matches** `.dv-legrow.is-solo` ✓ · `--ink` → `#1A1A1A` ✓ · `--line` → `#E1E1E1` ✓ · yet computed **`border-color` → `rgb(225,225,225)`** (= `--line`; the declaration did not win) and **`background` → `oklab(0 0 0 / 0)` = FULLY TRANSPARENT**, not 6% ink. **Control: the identical `color-mix` written literally on a sibling `<div>` in the SAME subtree → `color(srgb 0.101961 0.101961 0.101961 / 0.06)`** — the mix itself is fine. **A rule that matches, whose variables resolve, overridden on BOTH declarations.** The census enumerated only selectors containing `dv-legrow` (4, none able to beat `(0,2,0)`) ⇒ **the winning rule does not contain that string** — `:is()`/`:where()`, `.dv-leg > li`, `#cb4-legend li`, `!important`, inline/animated all plausible, **none checked. DO NOT GUESS IT.**
- **⚠⚠ CONSEQUENCE: DV-D17's RENDER-PROOF IS UNATTAINABLE AS SPECIFIED AND WOULD HAVE READ GREEN FOREVER.** The acceptance test is *"no `.dv-legrow` resolves the `.is-solo` treatment after isolate-then-check-on"*. **No row resolves it at ANY time** — before, during, or after isolation — so the assertion is permanently true for reasons unrelated to DV-D17, **and would have survived a complete revert of the fix.** ⇒ **DV-D17 stays ENACTED · DOM-PROVEN (108/108 + 27/27, three neutered controls) · RENDER-OWED.** ⚠ **Do NOT mark it render-verified on a green run of the probe as written.**
- **⚠ A QUESTION THAT IS DAVE'S, LOGGED NOT CHASED:** if the treatment has never painted, **the screenshot behind DV-D17 showed something** — either a context not yet probed, or the treatment worked once and regressed silently. Worth knowing which.
- **★ WHAT I GOT WRONG, AND IT IS THE TRANSFERABLE PART: THE FIRST RUN PRINTED `24 checks · 0 failures` AND WAS A FALSE GREEN.** The probe carried a positive control *precisely* to stop a vacuous pass — the solo row's paint must differ from baseline or the probe is blind. **It compared computed strings.** Solo bg serialises `oklab(0 0 0 / 0)`, baseline `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` — **textually different, visually identical (both fully transparent)** — so the control "saw a difference" and passed. **Caught BY EYE reading the JSON, not by the check.** ⇒ **Compare computed colours AS COLOURS (parse r/g/b/a), never as strings; a control that can pass on a serialisation difference is not a control.** ⚠ This happened *inside the probe written to honour* [[stale-reading-failure-mode]]'s sibling rule *"assume your probe is wrong in the direction that reads as green"* — **the rule was quoted in the file's own docstring and still did not prevent the failure it names**, because the control was checked for existence, not for sensitivity. Same shape as #7's stale-suite finding, and as ds-018/ds-019 themselves: **the instrument was present, correct-looking, and not measuring what it claimed.**
- **✅ HARNESS RE-PROVEN FROM COLD, runbook verbatim, no new steps** — pip→mount · TLS env · `chromium-headless-shell` (non-zero exit after a successful download = expected host-validation) · 11 libs via `apt-get download` · two-alias fontconfig · render. **NEW POTHOLE BANKED:** `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH` on the outputs mount ends the install with **`EPERM: operation not permitted, rmdir '__dirlock'`** — **same species as the host-validation exit: a failure message following a success. Check the cache, proceed.**

- **★ THE HEADLINE, and it is Dave's: INSTRUMENT FIT IS A THIRD AXIS.** Angle (1) of the FLOATED idea (*compare what a rule SAYS against what its gate DEMANDS*) **would NOT have caught `aid-009`.** The rule names *"a minimum 44×44px target area"*; the gate calls its check *"target size"* — **the vocabulary matches; the narrowing is in the INSTRUMENT.** A static regex observes a *declared box*; the rule is about a *target*. They stop tracking each other the moment the property is expressed through a mechanism (expander · token · transform) — and **the gate goes QUIET, not RED.** ⇒ **Sits ON TOP of ADR-0016, not inside it:** PROVEN/CLAIMED/UNPROVEN ask *"is there a check and can it fail?"*; this asks *"is it looking at the right thing?"* — **a check can be PROVEN and still measure a proxy that doesn't track its rule.** dv-004 was the *narrowing* case; `aid-009` is the *instrument* case, and it is larger.
- **★ ds-015 — ADOPTING THE CORRECT MECHANISM IS WHAT REMOVES A COMPONENT FROM THE CHECK.** `_validate_a11y.py` does not mis-measure the `::before` expander; it **`continue`s**, deferring to a "render axis" that **does not exist** (the hit-area gate is still PENDING DAVE SIGN-OFF). **MEASURED: 67 snippets · 1,869 selectors skipped by the `CTRL` vocabulary · 14 eligible · 7 exempted · 7 actually measured.** Verdict **0 failures, exit 0**; 64 of 67 snippets use `::before`. **The diamond proves it three times** (`.dv-leg-sw.sw-diamond`, 8×8 + `rotate(45deg)`): out of `CTRL` scope · expander is `min-width:var(--hit,44px)` and the regex reads literal `(\d+)px` · `::before` → exempt. ⚠ And its real defect is a **transform** consequence — **no static box measurement can see that in principle**; `elementFromPoint` caught it by hand in the legend wave.
- **★ DAVE'S SECOND REFRAME — the propagation defect, and the strongest angle yet.** *"we are relying too much on my memory here, the hit mechanism should have triggered something that cascaded this elsewhere."* Adoption is a LOCAL CSS decision with a GLOBAL governance consequence and **nothing carried it** — no registry entry, no re-check, no flag. Sibling of the assertion-propagation gap, but worse: **the gate never fires at all, because the adoption IS the exemption.** ⇒ **The trigger should be ADOPTION-TIME, not sweep-time.** Cheapest cut: make claiming the exemption require naming the rule it exempts from, and let the register harvest it — **an exemption that must declare itself is one you can count.**

- **★ DV-D14 — dv-004 is satisfied by GEOMETRY on gridded plots (Dave's ruling, against my recommendation).** I proposed the donut's 2px `stroke="var(--page)"`. Dave: *"I prefer the geometry the border will obscure gridlines, may I know why you recommend borders?"* **He was right and the check took one grep: `cb5` carries 5 full-width `.dv-grid` lines behind the columns; the donut carries none.** An SVG stroke straddles its path, so 2px puts 1px OUTSIDE each rect — painting over every gridline down both sides of all 4 columns. ⇒ **A surface-coloured stroke only simulates separation when the thing behind it IS the surface.** Enacted **variant A, both ends pinned**: baseline `y=230` and each stack top stay TRUE, the 4px comes out of segment heights. **Accepted cost, stated at ruling time: segments understate 2.0–2.6%, worst on the shortest column.**
- **★ THE FINDING THAT OUTRANKS THE CHART — the gate had narrowed the rule.** dv-004's text is **mechanism-NEUTRAL** (*"minimum 2px separation between colour blocks"*); `_validate_dataviz.py` enforced *"must carry a surface-coloured stroke >=2px"*. Both directions bite: a chart satisfying dv-004 by real geometry **would have FAILED**, and **an agent reading the gate to learn what compliance looks like gets the wrong answer** — which is exactly what happened to me. Dave supplied the governing fact from the KG (*"the gap is only 2px minimum, this is in the dataviz specifications in the KG"*) and then named the class himself → **held as FLOATED in `_FUTURE-STATE.md`** (his exploration, do not build ahead of it). ⚠ Caution recorded there: the consult prints `rulings (5/5 shown, --all for more)` — **it TRUNCATES**, so a forcing function built on it would be a CLAIMED gate with extra steps.
- **★ DV-D15 — `data/text/on-series` MINTED (Dave's promotion).** The keys had declared `var(--page)`: white today only because of where the neutral ramp sits — **a coincidence, not an intent**. New role → `color/grey/white`, **pinned in BOTH modes**, modelled on `rag/text/on-dark`; **deliberately NO alpha channel** (DV-D07 computes contrast from the composite, so an alpha slot is a route to passing on the hex and failing on screen). **type26-013 does NOT collide** — checked, not assumed: *"black/dark-grey on light · white/light-grey on dark"* covers it; the white-only clause is specific to RED grounds.
- **★ THE VOCABULARY HAD FORKED — fixed structurally, not by enumeration.** The corpus carries **both `stacked` and `stacked-column`, both `grouped` and `grouped-column`**, plus `scatter`. Adding three strings would have guaranteed a repeat ⇒ **`DTYPE_CANON` normalises once at read time** + **`dv-vocab` (NEW, BLOCKING) fails the build on any dtype the gate has never heard of**, naming the rules that would have skipped it. Scope-blindness audit: 3 blind values → **"none detected"**. **The dataviz selftest is now WIRED into the build** — it existed and ran only by hand, which is precisely why nothing ever proved dv-004 could fail. 9 new bites, incl. one reproducing the exact ds-014 figure.

- **★ PROVEN BY RENDER — `knowledge/_verify_dv_stacked_enactment.py` (NEW, the ADR-0016 P2 proof).** Licensed cut, **snippet AND showroom pane, 1180 AND 760, all four agreeing**: separation **2.00px** on all 8 boundaries; keys white at **5.26 / 5.04 / 4.61:1** vs AA 4.5. ⚠ **The prior handoff predicted "≈5:1"; MEASURED worst case is 4.61:1** (series-3, margin **0.11**) — recorded as measured. **Series-3 cannot be lightened without breaking AA.**

## ⏱ PRIOR DELTA — 2026-07-27 (Mon **later morning #4**, OPUS solo self-conducting, effort MAX — ★ **CALLS (1)(2)(3)(5) RULED BY DAVE; the 465-rule INSTRUMENT-FIT pass is BUILT, RUN and WIRED** · ★ **ds-016 — 7 LIVE GATES CITE RULES THE INDEX CANNOT SEE: 698 anchors declared, 465 indexed, 265 invisible — `aid-009` among them** · build **60/60 GREEN**; 🔴 RED ~72% at ritual) — "He said 'most robust methods', so the tool was built so it could not guess. Then bite 1 failed on its first run and the failure was the finding: the rule the a11y gate enforces is not in the rules index at all."

- **★ CALL (3) DELIVERED — `knowledge/_build_instrument_fit.py`, the instrument ladder at corpus scale.** I0 STATIC (source bytes) → I1 DOM (parsed tree) → I2 RENDER (computed/composited/geometric) → I3 EYE (judgment, never a defect). **Sits ON TOP of ADR-0016:** the register asks *is there a check and can it fail*; this asks *can it observe the property at all*. Generated `_INSTRUMENT-FIT.md` + `_instrument-fit.json`, **11 bites**, wired **advisory** as build steps 58 (selftest) + 59 (report) per the P3 posture.
- **★ MEASURED: 465 rules — UNDER-INSTRUMENTED 12 · UNGATED 165 · UNTAGGED 279 · EYE-ONLY 4 · FIT 5.** ⚠ **The limit is stated first, not buried: 279 of 465 (60%) are UNTAGGED**, so **12 is a FLOOR, not an answer.** Deliberately NOT fixed by widening patterns until the report went quiet — that is anti-false-fix #2 in the tool's own header, and it would convert a measured finding into a tuned one. **Two of the 12 are BLOCKING and both are contrast rules with STATIC gates:** `dv-016` (chart titles/axis/gridlines) and `icon-011` (*"icons require 4.5:1 in all instances"*). **Contrast is a COMPOSITED property** — a static parse reads the declared hex, not what lands on screen. Same class as ds-010 · ds-013 · DV-D15, now four proven instances.
- **★ ds-016 — THE FINDING NOBODY WENT LOOKING FOR, and it is ds-015 INVERTED.** Bite 1 asserted the ds-015 ground truth and returned `None`: **`aid-009` is not in `_rules-index.json`.** Chasing it: `guidelines/*.md` declares **698** rule anchors; the index holds the **465** carrying an enforcement-destiny tag; the other **265 are invisible** to the index, `_consult.py`, the enactment register and this pass — and **7 of them are cited by a live gate as its authority** (`aca-003` · `aca-004` · **`aid-009`** · `aid-020` · `avd-006` · `axs-003` · `nam-001`). `aid-009` is Dave's 2026-07-03 hit-area ruling, named 5× by `_validate_a11y.py`; **its anchor line simply never got a destiny tag.** Mirror image in the same family: **`icon-005` (BLOCKING, 44×44 target) IS indexed and NO gate names it.** ⇒ There the gate couldn't see the component; **here the INDEX can't see the rule** — markup correct, lookup fails, nothing reports it. ⚠ **The 265 are excluded BY DESIGN, not by bug; the defect is the SILENCE.** LOGGED not fixed — the remedy is call (4)'s discussion, unruled.
- **⚠ THE BITES CAUGHT THREE DEFECTS IN MY OWN TOOL, ALL ON FIRST RUN.** (1) A crude bare-word probe read **three** gates as RENDER — `_validate_proforma.py`, `_validate_token_tiers.py`, `_validate_type_composites.py` — matching the word **chrome**, as in *mono*chrome and *demo-*chrome. ⚠ **Wrong direction: it makes gates look STRONGER than they are**, which in a fitness audit buys silence. (2) `icon-005` came out **FIT** with `_build_instrument_fit.py` itself as the evidence — **a register that NAMES a rule was counting as a check that ENFORCES it**; fixed with `NOT_A_GATE` (this file + `_build_enactment_register.py`, same exposure). (3) bite 1's failure ⇒ ds-016. **Third session running for "assume your probe is wrong in the direction that reads as green".**
- **★ CALL (2) RULED — ADOPTION-TIME AND SWEEP ARE COMPLEMENTARY (Dave: *"You're right about 2 they are complimentary"*).** He asked to be corrected; **his lean was right, the framing was the thing that needed correcting.** Adoption-time captures intent from whoever knows *why* — **counted** data, not inferred — but is blind to everything already shipped (**64 of 67 snippets already carry the `::before` expander; it would find zero**), and a *missing* declaration is silence. Sweep is retroactive and needs no cooperation — **proven the same day, it found the 7 dangling citations laid down months apart** — but reports late and **its own coverage is unmeasured** (blind to 265 rules, can't classify 279 more; nothing revealed that until a second check was built). ⇒ **The only way to detect a MISSING declaration is to sweep for it**, so the sweep is what makes adoption-time enforceable. Held shape: **adoption-time forcing function + a sweep narrowed to one job, finding undeclared adoptions.**
- **Evidence:** build `python3 knowledge/_build_all.py` **60/60 GREEN exit 0** · 2026-07-27 · selftest `_build_instrument_fit.py --selftest` **11/11** · 2026-07-27 · register `knowledge/_INSTRUMENT-FIT.md` (465 rules) · 2026-07-27 · capture gate **13 in scope / 0 fail / 0 warn** · 2026-07-27 · defect `knowledge/_DS-IMPROVEMENTS.md` **ds-016** · ideas `_FUTURE-STATE.md` § KG forcing-function **Exploration beat 2** · dossier `_DECISION-HISTORY/2026-07-27-the-index-cannot-see-the-rule.md`.
- **⬛ STILL DAVE'S:** **(4)** consult enforcement column — *"I lean fix, but this probably needs a discussion"*, **NOT ruled**; the `5/5 shown` denominator is separable + trivial and was **NOT done** (Red budget). **(5)** `CTRL` vocabulary sweep — **ruled YES, NOT started.** **NEW:** invest in the pattern table (279 UNTAGGED)? · ds-016's three remedies (fail-loud / tag the 7 / both).
- **🔴 Gauge: RED ~72% at ritual (ESTIMATE ±15%).** Read off the band table. Pre-flight was stated before the build and ran ~10 points over — the miss was ds-016, an unplanned finding chased mid-job. **Wrap reserved in advance per the three-term rule.**
